# All Difficulty-Vector Combinations Within Probability Bands

**Status:** Phase 1 research artifact; exhaustive exact multinomial/thinning-DP calculation.

**Bands:** Easy 80–95%; Medium 60–75%; Hard 40–55%.

**Thresholds:** DC 2 through DC 12 only.

**Vectors:** Every length from one threshold through the full Dice Pool length. Repeated thresholds are retained as meaningful requirements; internally, repeated values are handled by the required-count state.

The calculation uses one depth-first traversal per pool profile. Each child vector receives the parent’s exact thinning-DP state, and branches below the 40% cutoff are pruned. Exact rational arithmetic makes band-boundary inclusion deterministic.
